## i left one event as example for the rest of the events, if you're not sure check out KR code

add_namespace = annex

##   C E N T R A L   E U R O P E   ##

country_event = { #Fate of France
	id = annex.1001
	title = annexation_16
	desc = annexation_16_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 17 #Meurthe
							state = 29 #Pas-de-Calais
							state = 31 #Roussillon
							state = 685 #Champagne Ardenne
							state = 735 #Savoy
							state = 809 #Franche Comte
							state = 855 #Dunkerque Corridor
							state = 1206 #Moselle
							state = 1207 #Verdun
						}
					} 
					remove_core_of = FRA
					retain_state = yes
				}
				remove_claim_by = FRA
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet (Almost) Entire France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.1001.a"
		name = annex.1001.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_france = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = FRA }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = FRA }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = { 
			limit = { ROOT = { has_custom_puppet_FRA = yes } }
			setup_custom_puppet_FRA = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #France Delenda Est
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.1001.b"
		name = annex.1001.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_france = yes
			}
		}

		set_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
		custom_effect_tooltip = annexations_country_to_be_divided_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = FRA
					NOT = {
						state = 16 #Paris
						state = 24 #Centre
						state = 785 #Picardy
						state = 18 #Champagne
					}
				}
				remove_core_of = FRA
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = FRA }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Monarchy_Split
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Republic_Split
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		FRA = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = FRA }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Occitania
	id = annex.1002
	title = annexation_1202
	desc = annexation_1202_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = {
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Occitania
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.1002.a"
		name = annex.1002.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = OCT }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Burgundy
	id = annex.1003
	title = annexation_27
	desc = annexation_27_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_burgundy = no }
					remove_core_of = BRG
				}
				else = {
					add_core_of = BRG
				}
			}
			every_state = { 
				if = { 
					limit = { NOT = { state = 27 } } #Garonne
					retain_state = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					FRA = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Burgundy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.1003.a"
		name = annex.1003.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BRG }
		27 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Bourgogne
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			BRG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BRG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			BRG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BRG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		27 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Bourgogne
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		27 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Bourgogne
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		27 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Bourgogne
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Normandy
	id = annex.1004
	title = annexation_15
	desc = annexation_15_desc

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {

				FRA = {

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = NRM }
		15 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Normandy
		1204 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Seine-Maritime

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
			}

			base = 100

				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1

			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		15 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Normandy
		1204 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Seine-Maritime
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1004 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

country_event = { #Fate of Brittany
	id = annex.1005
	title = annexation_14
	desc = annexation_14_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = { 
			limit = { 
				OR = { 
					tag = FRA
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = {
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Brittany
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1005 option annex.1005.a"
		name = annex.1005.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BRT }
		14 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } 
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			BRT = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BRT_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			BRT = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BRT_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1004 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		14 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Brittany
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1004 option annex.give_to_overlord"

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }

country_event = { #Fate of Loire
	id = annex.1006
	title = annexation_30
	desc = annexation_30_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					14 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Brittany
					24 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Centre
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				24 = { #Centre
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:24.owner = { save_event_target_as = france }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				14 = { #Brittany
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:france }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:14.owner = { save_event_target_as = brittany }
		}
	}

	option = { #give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.europe.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_france = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				is_ally_with = event_target:brittany
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		30 = { #Loire
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes 
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #give to Brittany
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.1006.a"
		name = annex.1006.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = brittany
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:brittany }
				is_ally_with = event_target:france
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:brittany = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:brittany }
		30 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Loire
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		30 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Loire
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		30 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Loire
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}


country_event = { #Fate of Calais
	id = annex.1007
	title = annexation_29
	desc = annexation_29_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					785 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Picardy
					855 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Dunkerque
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				785 = { #Picardy
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:785.owner = { save_event_target_as = picardy }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				855 = { #Dunkerque
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:picardy }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:855.owner = { save_event_target_as = dunkerque }
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to owner of Picardy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1007 option annex.1007.a"
		name = annex.1007.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = picardy
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:picardy }
				is_ally_with = event_target:dunkerque
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = dunkerque }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:picardy = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:picardy }
		29 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #
	}
	option = { #Give to owner of Dunkerque
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1007 option annex.1007.b"
		name = annex.1007.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = dunkerque
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:dunkerque }
				is_ally_with = event_target:picardy
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = picardy }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:dunkerque = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:dunkerque }
		29 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Dunkerque
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1007 option name annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		29 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Dunkerque
	}

	option = { #Annex

		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		29 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Dunkerque
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1007 option name annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}


}

country_event = { #Fate of Britain
	id = annex.1013
	title = annexation_126
	desc = annexation_126_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_great_britain = yes }
					add_core_of = ENG
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = ENG
				}
				remove_claim_by = ENG
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.1013.a"
		name = annex.1013.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_britain = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ENG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_ENG = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_ENG = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Split Up Great Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.1013.b"
		name = annex.1013.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_britain = yes
			}
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = annexations_country_to_be_divided_tt

		set_global_flag = PB_ENG_Divided_via_Peace


		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ENG }

		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_england = no
					} 
					remove_core_of = ENG
				}
				remove_claim_by = ENG
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ENG }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ENG_puppet_tooltip
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ENG }
			release_targeted_tag = yes
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Monarchy_Split
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Republic_Split
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		ENG = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = ENG }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Wales
	id = annex.1014

	desc = annexation_122_desc

		}

			}

			}

					has_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Monarchy
				}
			}
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Monarchy_with_Wales
			}
		}
	}

			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

			limit = { is_wales = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}

country_event = { #Fate of Portugal
	id = annex.1016
	title = annexation_112
	desc = annexation_112_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_Generic_Art_Gallery

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = POR } }
				POR = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = PEF } }
				PEF = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = POR
						original_tag = PEF
						original_tag = SPR
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			random_other_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = POR
						original_tag = PEF
					}
					is_ally_with = ROOT
					OR = {
						is_subject = no
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = portugal
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = portugal } }
				if = {
					limit = { POR = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					POR = { save_event_target_as = portugal }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { PEF = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					PEF = { save_event_target_as = portugal }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = spain } }
				if = {
					limit = { SPR = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					SPR = { save_event_target_as = spain }
				}
			}
			171 = { retain_state = yes } #Galicia
		}
	}

	option = { #Restore portuguese empire
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1016 option annex.1016.a"
		name = annex.1016.a

			NOT = { country_exists = PEF }

		}

			base = 100

				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_release_puppet_default_logic = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PEF }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_portugal = yes
				NOT = { is_core_of = var:tag_to_release }
			}
			add_claim_by = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag_with_claims = yes
	}

	option = { #Restore portuguese monarchy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1016 option annex.1016.b"
		name = annex.1016.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = { country_exists = POR }
			NOT = {
				original_tag = POR
				original_tag = PEF
			}
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_release_puppet_default_logic = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = POR }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_portugal = yes
				NOT = { is_core_of = var:tag_to_release }
			}
			add_claim_by = var:tag_to_release
		}

	option = { #Give to Porugal

				}

		}

		}

			modifier = {

			}

			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }

				set_cosmetic_tag = SPR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SPR = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SPR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

			annexations_reset_italy = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = PAP
						AND = {
							is_italian_tag = yes
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {

						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}

			}
			annexations_create_italian_targets = yes
		}
	}

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = papacy
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:papal_states }

				is_ally_with = event_target:italy_soc
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PAP }

		1432 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Umbria

				}

country_event = { #Fate of Hesse
	id = annex.1041
	title = annexation_1185
	desc = annexation_1185_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_german_tags = yes
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_germany = yes 
					}
					add_core_of = GER
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = GER
					remove_claim_by = GER
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_german_unifier_tag = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}

				limit = { annexations_germany_is_independent_ally_or_puppet_of_ROOT = no }

							annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
							owner = {
								NOT = { tag = event_target:germany }
								ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							}
						}
					}
					var:52.owner = { save_event_target_as = bavaria add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						57 = { #_pphalia
							annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes

								NOT = { tag = event_target:germany }

				factor = 0

			}
		}
		annexations_release_MGE_germany = yes
	}

		name = annex.1041.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_germany = yes
			}
		}

	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = AUS
					original_tag = PRS
					original_tag = ITA
					original_tag = IST
					is_german_tag = yes
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				1185 = { #Frankfurt
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						is_subject = no
						original_tag = GER
					}
				}
			}
			var:1185.owner = { save_event_target_as = germany }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				4 = { #Lower Austria
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						is_subject = no
						original_tag = AUS
					}
				}
			}
			var:4.owner = { save_event_target_as = austria }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				160 = { #Veneto
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						is_subject = no
					}
				}
			}
			var:160.owner = { save_event_target_as = veneto }
		}
	}

	option = { #Return to Germany
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1052 option annex.1052.a"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_germany
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = germany
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_GLOBAL_Germany_is_kil
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:germany }
		923 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Friaul
	}

	option = { #Give it to the owner of Veneto
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1052 option annex.1052.b"
		name = annex.1052.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = veneto
		}

		ai_chance = { base = 0 }

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:veneto }
		923 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Friaul

	}

	option = { #Return to Austria
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1052 option annex.1052.a"

	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1047 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				#is_starting_french_state = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate

		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}

			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1047 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Basque Country
	id = annex.1058
	title = annexation_172
	desc = annexation_172_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = SPR
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				176 = { #Castille
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						is_subject = no
					}
				}
			}
			var:176.owner = { save_event_target_as = spain }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Basque
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1058 option annex.1058.a"
		name = annex.1058.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = { factor = 0 }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BSQ }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			BSQ = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BSQ_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			BSQ = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Return it to Spain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1058 option annex.1058.b"
		name = annex.1058.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = spain
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0 
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:spain }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:spain = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SPR }
		172 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Basque Country
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1058 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1058 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		172 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes }
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1058 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = { recheck_annexations = yes }
}

						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:99.owner = { save_event_target_as = jutland }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					58 = { #Holstein
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							NOT = { tag = event_target:holstein }
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:58.owner = { save_event_target_as = holstein }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Holstein
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1060 option annex.1060.a"
		name = annex.1060.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { 
				SCH = { 
					exists = yes 
				}
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = DEN
					original_tag = GER
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SCH }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_SCH = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_SCH = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}	

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SCH = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SCH_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SCH = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SCH_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Return them to Jutland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1063 option annex.1063.a"
		name = annex.1063.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = jutland
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:jutland }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:holstein
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:jutland = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:jutland }
		1467 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #South Schleswig
		795 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #North Schleswig
	}

	option = { #Return them to Holstein
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1063 option annex.1063.b"
		name = annex.1063.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_event_target = holstein
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:holstein }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:jutland
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:holstein = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:holstein }
		1467 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #South Schleswig
		795 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #North Schleswig

country_event = { #Fate of Croatia
	id = annex.1071
	title = annexation_109
	desc = annexation_109_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		annexations_reset_yugoslavian_cores_claims = yes
		1235 = { retain_state = yes }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						794 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Lika
						107 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Belgrade
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Croatia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1071 option annex.1071.a"
		name = annex.1071.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				original_tag = SER
				original_tag = GER
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				original_tag = HWA
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CRO }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_CRO = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_CRO = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			CRO = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SLA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			CRO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Lay the foundations for a Yugoslav State
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1071 option annex.europe.form_yugoslavia"
		name = annex.europe.form_yugoslavia
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			is_major = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				original_tag = RUS
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CRO }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		1235 = { clear_retain_state = yes }

		45 = { retain_state = yes }
		764 = { retain_state = yes }
		163 = { retain_state = yes }
		1231 = { retain_state = yes }
		108 = { retain_state = yes }
		106 = { retain_state = yes }

		every_state = {
			limit = { is_yugoslavia = yes }
			add_claim_of_target = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_yugoslavia = yes
				}
			}
			transfer_state_without_overriding_occupation = yes
		}
		
		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_YUG = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_YUG = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			CRO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SER_Yugoslav_Empire
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					AND = {
						has_republican_government = yes 
						NOT = {
							has_government = jc
						}
					} 
				}
			}
			CRO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SER_Yugoslav_Federation
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					AND = {
						has_republican_government = yes
						has_government = jc
					}
				}
			}
			CRO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SER_Yugoslav_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Transfer it to the Dinarics
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1071 option annex.1071.b"
		name = annex.1071.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			has_monarchist_government = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = GER
					original_tag = HWA
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SLA }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				is_croatia = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Transfer it to Serbia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1071 option annex.1071.c"
		name = annex.1071.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			SER = {
				exists = yes
				tag_is_yugoslavia = yes 
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = RUS
						is_ally_with = SER
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SER }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				is_croatia = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1071 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }

	}

			}
			SER = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SER_Yugoslav_Federation
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					AND = {
						has_republican_government = yes
						has_government = jc
					}
				}
			}
			SER = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SER_Yugoslav_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1073 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				is_serbia = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1073 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_serbia = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1073 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Hungary
	id = annex.1080
	title = annexation_43
	desc = annexation_43_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			every_owned_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_hungary = yes
					}
					add_core_of = HUN
				}
				else = { 
					remove_core_of = HUN
					remove_claim_by = HUN
				}
			}
			45 = { retain_state = yes }
			764 = { retain_state = yes }
			82 = { retain_state = yes }
			83 = { retain_state = yes }
			76 = { retain_state = yes }
			1238 = { retain_state = yes }
			1035 = { retain_state = yes }
			1034 = { retain_state = yes }
			1033 = { retain_state = yes }
			84 = { retain_state = yes }
			664 = { retain_state = yes }
			73 = { retain_state = yes }
			1241 = { retain_state = yes }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Hungary
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1080 option annex.1080.a"
		name = annex.1080.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = HUN }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_HUN = yes }
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
			setup_custom_puppet_HUN = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = GER
					}
				}
			}
			HUN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = HUN_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = { 
					has_republican_government = yes 
				} 
			}
			HUN = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1080 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_hungary = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1080 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_hungary = yes }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1080 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Wallachia
	id = annex.1086
	title = annexation_46
	desc = annexation_46_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
            limit = {
				is_subject = no
                OR = {
                    original_tag = MOL
                    original_tag = BUL
                }
            }
            save_event_target_as = can_annex
        }
	}

	option = { #Release Wallachia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1086 option annex.1086.a"
		name = annex.1086.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				original_tag = MOL
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ROM }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_ROM = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_ROM = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}	

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ROM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ROM_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}


		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			ROM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = ROM_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Return it to Moldova
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1086 option annex.1086.b"
		name = annex.1086.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			MOL = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = RUS
					original_tag = BUL
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 100
				ROOT = {
					NOT = {
						original_tag = RUS
						original_tag = BUL
					}
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = MOL
						has_subject = MOL
					}
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MOL }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				is_wallachia = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
		
		MOL = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MOL_ROM_Unity_Monarchy
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_republican_government = yes
				}
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MOL_ROM_Unity_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1086 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
				is_wallachia = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1086 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_wallachia = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1086 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Greece
	id = annex.1095
	title = annexation_47
	desc = annexation_47_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		731 = { retain_state = yes }
		182 = { retain_state = yes }
		184 = { retain_state = yes }
		185 = { retain_state = yes }
		821 = { retain_state = yes }
		822 = { retain_state = yes }
		339 = { retain_state = yes }
		823 = { retain_state = yes }
		1043 = { retain_state = yes }
		1046 = { retain_state = yes }
		164 = { retain_state = yes }
	}

	option = { #Release Greece
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1095 option annex.1095.a"
		name = annex.1095.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = GRE }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			GRE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = GRE_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}


		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			GRE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = GRE_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1095 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_greece = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1095 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_greece = yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1095 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Sardinia
	id = annex.1099
	title = annexation_114
	desc = annexation_114_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_italy = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_italian_tag = yes }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			annexations_create_italian_targets = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Sardinia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1099 option annex.1099.a"
		name = annex.1099.a
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_italian_splinters = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SRD }
		114 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Sardinia
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SRD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SRD_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SRD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SRD_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

	}

	option = { #Puppet Sardinia-Piedmont
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1099 option annex.1099.b"
		name = annex.1099.b
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_italian_splinters = yes
			OR = {
				AND = {
					SAV = {
						exists = yes
						owns_state = 158 #Piedmont
						owns_state = 1023 #Liguria
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
				AND = {
					SAV = {
						exists = no 
					}
					ROOT = {
						owns_state = 158 #Piedmont
						owns_state = 1023 #Liguria
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SRD }
		114 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Sardinia
		SAV = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SRD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SRD_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SRD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SRD_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

	}

	option = { #Give to Italy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1099 option annex.europe.give_to_italy"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_italy
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_event_target = italy
				has_event_target = italy_soc
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy 
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy_soc
					}
				}
			}
		}
		annexations_give_italy_to_target = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1099 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		114 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Sardinia
	}

	option = { #Integrate

		}

	}

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {

	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Poland
	id = annex.1108
	title = annexation_10
	desc = annexation_10_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { 
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_poland = yes 
					}
					add_core_of = POL
				}
				else = {
					remove_core_of = POL
					remove_claim_by = POL
				}
			}
		}
		86 = { retain_state = yes } #Wielpolska
		91 = { retain_state = yes } #Lwow
		1402 = { retain_state = yes } #Tarnopol
		89 = { retain_state = yes } #Stanislawow
		80 = { retain_state = yes } #Bucovina
		1036 = { retain_state = yes } #Southern Bucovina
		88 = { retain_state = yes } #Kraków
		94 = { retain_state = yes } #Brest-Litovsk
		1436 = { retain_state = yes } #Pinsk
		93 = { retain_state = yes } #Volhynia
		67 = { retain_state = yes } #Oberschliesen
		762 = { retain_state = yes } #Katowice
		97 = { retain_state = yes } #Bialyostok
		95 = { retain_state = yes } #Nowogordek
		784 = { retain_state = yes } #Vilnius
		96 = { retain_state = yes } #Wilijeka
	}

	option = { #Release Poland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1108 option annex.1108.a"
		name = annex.1108.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = POL }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = GER
					}
				}
			}
			POL = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = POL_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = GER
				}
			}
			POL = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = POL_Occupied_Monarchy_Kraut
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			POL = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = POL_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1108 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_poland =yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1108 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_poland =yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1108 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Prussia
	id = annex.1109
	title = annexation_prussia
	desc = annexation_prussia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		every_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_prussia = yes 
				}
				add_core_of = PRS
			}
			else = {
				remove_core_of = PRS
				remove_claim_by = PRS
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Prussia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1109 option annex.1109.a"
		name = annex.1109.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 10
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = ENG
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				ROOT = {
					original_tag = GER
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PRS }
		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = { 
			limit = { ROOT = { has_custom_puppet_PRS = yes  } } 
			setup_custom_puppet_PRS = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
				}
			}
			PRS = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PRS_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			PRS = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PRS_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1109 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_prussia =yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1109 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_prussia =yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1109 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

			base = 10
		}

				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = RUS_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			RUS = {

				set_cosmetic_tag = RUS_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_russia =yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Ukraine
	id = annex.1117
	title = annexation_ukraine
	desc = annexation_ukraine_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		every_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_ukraine = yes 
				}
				add_core_of = UKR
			}
			else = {
				remove_core_of = UKR
				remove_claim_by = UKR
			}
		}
		78 = { retain_state = yes } #Bessarabia
		766 = { retain_state = yes } #Akkerman
		1208 = { retain_state = yes } #Gagauzia
		137 = { retain_state = yes } #Crimea

		225 = { retain_state = yes } #Glukhov
	}

		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1117 option annex.1117.a"
		name = annex.1117.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

			base = 10
		}

		release_targeted_tag = yes
		if = { 
			limit = { has_custom_puppet_UKR = yes }
			setup_custom_puppet_UKR = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_custom_puppet
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			UKR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = UKR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			UKR = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = UKR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1117 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_ukraine =yes
				is_valid_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1117 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = { 
			limit = { 
				is_ukraine =yes
			}
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1117 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:pinsk }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:chelm
					is_ally_with = event_target:zhitomir
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:pinsk = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:pinsk }
		94 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Brest Litovsk
		93 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Volhynia
	}

	option = { #Grant the land to chelm
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1122 option annex.1122.b"
		name = annex.1122.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = chelm
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:chelm }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:pinsk
					is_ally_with = event_target:zhitomir
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:chelm = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:chelm }
		94 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Brest Litovsk
		93 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Volhynia
	}

	option = { #Grant the land to zhitomir
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1122 option annex.1122.c"
		name = annex.1122.c
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = zhitomir
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:zhitomir }
				OR = {
					is_ally_with = event_target:pinsk
					is_ally_with = event_target:chelm
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:zhitomir = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:zhitomir }
		94 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Brest Litovsk
		93 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Volhynia
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1115 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		94 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Brest Litovsk
		93 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Volhynia
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1115 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		94 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Brest Litovsk
		93 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Volhynia
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1122 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = { 
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Volga Germany
	id = annex.1123
	title = annexation_volga_germany
	desc = annexation_volga_germany_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
	}

	option = { #Release an independent Volga Germany
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1123 option annex.1123.a"
		name = annex.1123.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = VGE }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = GER
				}
			}
			VGE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = VGE_Occupied_Monarchy_Kraut
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = GER
					}
				}
			}
			VGE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = VGE_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			VGE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = VGE_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give it back to Russia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1123 option annex.1123.b"
		name = annex.1123.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			RUS = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		RUS = {
			transfer_state = 401
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate it
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1123 option annex.1123.c"
		name = annex.1123.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			OR = {
				original_tag = GER
				original_tag = PRS
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		ROOT = {
			add_state_core = 401
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1123 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

	}

country_event = { #Fate of Georgia
	id = annex.1131
	title = annexation_georgia
	desc = annexation_georgia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		every_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_georgia = no }
				remove_core_of = GEO
			}
			else = {
				add_core_of = GEO
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release an independent Georgia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1131 option annex.1131.a"
		name = annex.1131.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = GEO }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			GEO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = GEO_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			GEO = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = GEO_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1131 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		GEO = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1131 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		GEO = { add_claim_of_ROOT_on_valid_cores = yes }
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1131 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Doggerland
	id = annex.1135
	title = annexation_1421
	desc = annexation_1421_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = ENG
						original_tag = HOL
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ENG = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				ENG = {
					save_event_target_as = britain
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { HOL = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				HOL = {
					save_event_target_as = netherlands
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { FRA = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				FRA = {
					save_event_target_as = france
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Doggerland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1135 option annex.1135.a"
		name = annex.1135.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				NOT = {
					original_tag = ENG
					original_tag = HOL
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = -2
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				OR = {
					original_tag = ENG
					original_tag = HOL
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = DGG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes original_tag = ENG } }
			DGG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DGG_League_Mandate
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes NOT = { original_tag = ENG } } }
			DGG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DGG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			DGG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DGG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1135 option annex.europe.give_to_britain"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_britain
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = britain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:britain }
				126 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #London
				7 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #Holland
				OR = {
					is_major = yes
					is_ally_with = event_target:france
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.05
				event_target:britain = { is_ally_of_ROOT = yes }
			}

				}

			else_if = {

				IST = {
					save_event_target_as = italy
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_event_target = italy }

						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes

							NOT = { tag = event_target:britain }

		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1147 option annex.1147.a"
		name = annex.1147.a

			FIN = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FIN_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			FIN = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FIN_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Return to Sweden

		trigger = {
			has_event_target = sweden
	    }

			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
        set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:sweden }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_finland = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give it to Russia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1147 option annex.1147.b"
		name = annex.1147.c
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = russia

				factor = 0

			}
		}

		}

	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1147 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord

			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes

		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SCL }

				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }

			}
		}

			base = 80

			}

				}

	id = annex.1186
	title = annexation_84
	desc = annexation_84_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						154 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Alfold
						1037 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Ploiesti
						79 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Iasi
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					154 = { #Alfold
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}
				var:154.owner = { save_event_target_as = alfold }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					1037 = { #Ploiesti
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
						owner = {
							ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						}
					}
				}

			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						79 = { #Iasi
							is_owned_by = ROM
							is_owned_by = MOL
							annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
							owner = {
								ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							}
						}
						46 = { #Muntenia
							is_owned_by = ROM
							is_owned_by = MOL

							owner = {
								ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				var:79.owner = { save_event_target_as = iasi }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Transylvania
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1186 option annex.1186.a"
		name = annex.1186.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			TRA = {
				exists = no
			}
			NOT = {
				original_tag = ROM
				original_tag = MOL
				original_tag = HUN
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = TRA }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			TRA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = TRA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			TRA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = TRA_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give the land to Alfold
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1186 option annex.1186.b"
		name = annex.1186.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = alfold
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:alfold }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_east_transylvania = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes

						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						NOT = { tag = event_target:austria }
						NOT = { tag = event_target:italy }
					}
				}
			}
			var:3.owner = { save_event_target_as = swiss }
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to owner of Tyrol
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1197 option annex.1197.a"
		name = annex.1197.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = austria
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:austria }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:swiss
					is_ally_with = event_target:italy
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:austria = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:austria }
		971 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Geneve and Vaud
	}

	option = { #Give to owner of Lombardy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1197 option annex.1197.b"
		name = annex.1197.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = italy
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:italy }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:swiss
					is_ally_with = event_target:austria
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:italy = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:italy }
		971 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Geneve and Vaud
	}


	option = { #Give to owner of Switzerland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1197 option annex.1197.c"
		name = annex.1197.c
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = swiss
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:swiss }
				OR = { 
					is_ally_with = event_target:italy
					is_ally_with = event_target:austria
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:swiss = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:swiss }
		971 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Geneve and Vaud
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1197 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		971 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Geneve and Vaud
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1197 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		971 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Geneve and Vaud
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1197 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}
